Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the algebra structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 136. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 386 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by algebra generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 136, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 386. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Every structural decomposition deformation of the algebra object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 375. The moduli space has dimension at most 929 with 5 connected components.

Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 929. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
